TRIGGER: 사용자가 srnd.snu.ac.kr에 있거나, SRnD/연구행정을 언급하거나, 과제조회·지출신청·참여연구원·연구비·카드청구·연구업적·구매·출장신청 등 서울대 연구행정 업무를 요청할 때. DO NOT TRIGGER: 다른 대학 시스템이나 일반 웹앱을 다룰 때. ([hyeshik/qbio-skills](https://github.com/hyeshik/qbio-skills/tree/HEAD/snu-srnd) · ★28)
- **snu-submit**: 서울대 eTL 과제 제출 직전 점검. 학생이 과제 파일(ZIP·PDF·Word·PowerPoint·Excel·한글 HWPX·Python 등)을 주며 '이대로 제출해도 돼?', '빠진 거 없어?'처럼 물으면, 로그인된 Chrome의 eTL에서 그 과제의 안내·관련 공지·첨부를 읽어 제출 요건을 모으고 파일의 실제 내용과 대조해 PASS / PASS_WITH_LIMITS / 수정 필요 / 확인 필요와 고칠 점을 한국어로 알려 준다. 누락 파일, 빈 답·빈 파일, 형식·파일명·쪽수 위반, 공지로 바뀐 요건을 찾는다. 업로드·제출·채점·원본 수정은 하지 않는다. ([dong6288/snu-submit](https://github.com/dong6288/snu-submit/tree/HEAD/plugins/snu-submit/skills/snu-submit))

- **snug**: A Snug app is ONE self-contained HTML file. It runs in a sandboxed runner, keeps its state through the runner's storage, and MAY think through the user's own agent at runtime — a chess game asks for a move, a tutor asks for feedback, a tracker asks a question about its own data. The user owns the app and everything it accumulates: both live in one file the user keeps, and the runner supplies the i ([snugprotocol/snug](https://github.com/snugprotocol/snug/tree/HEAD/packages/knowledge/prompts/skills/snug) · ★2)
- **snug-asset-handling**: How to reference user-supplied images, videos, and audio inside a Snug Remotion composition — Snug copies uploaded media into `public/snug-assets/`, and you reference them with `staticFile("snug-assets/&lt;name&gt;")`. Load this whenever a composition needs a user-provided asset, the user uploads media, or you are tempted to use an external URL or absolute path. Do NOT use this for asset URLs that are intentionally remote (CDN images that the user explicitly asked for). ([mellofordev/snug](https://github.com/mellofordev/snug/tree/HEAD/packages/scaffold/templates/remotion/.claude/skills/snug-asset-handling) · 2 copies)
- **snug-composition-patterns**: Recipe book of canonical Snug video skeletons — text intro, kinetic typography, image reveal, lower-third, product demo, end-card. Each recipe is a complete, working `compositions/&lt;Name&gt;.tsx` file you can drop in and adapt. Load this when the user asks for a kind of video that fits one of the recipes, or when starting a fresh composition from a vague brief. Do NOT use this for upstream Remotion API reference (use `remotion-best-practices`) or for the Snug project rules (use `snug-scaffold`). ([mellofordev/snug](https://github.com/mellofordev/snug/tree/HEAD/packages/scaffold/templates/remotion/.claude/skills/snug-composition-patterns) · 2 copies)
- **snug-preview-and-render**: How preview and render work in a Snug Remotion project — Snug owns the Vite player and the render command; the agent never starts the player or invokes `bun run render` directly. Outputs land at `output/snug-out-N.mp4`. Load this when the user asks how to preview, how to render, why a composition isn't appearing, or when you are about to invoke a CLI command. Do NOT use this for upstream Remotion CLI flags (those live in `remotion-best-practices`). ([mellofordev/snug](https://github.com/mellofordev/snug/tree/HEAD/packages/scaffold/templates/remotion/.claude/skills/snug-preview-and-render) · 2 copies)
- **snug-scaffold**: Snug's project contract for a Remotion scaffold — file-name = composition id, default-export rule, allowed metadata exports, framework-owned files (do-not-edit list), and how compositions are auto-discovered by Remotion's CLI bundle and the Vite player. Load this whenever you are about to create or edit a file in a Snug Remotion project. Do NOT use this for Remotion API questions (use `remotion-best-practices`) or for video-style recipes (use `snug-composition-patterns`). ([mellofordev/snug](https://github.com/mellofordev/snug/tree/HEAD/packages/scaffold/templates/remotion/.claude/skills/snug-scaffold) · 2 copies)

- **snv**: Guide agents through Node identity, resource, cluster, and naming questions without allowing one subvector to absorb another. ([QuanuX/Symphony](https://github.com/QuanuX/Symphony/tree/HEAD/knowledge/snv) · ★2)
- **snv-accuracy-evaluation-against-ground-truth**: Use when you have simulated long-read (PacBio SMS) reads with a known variant truth set and need to quantify SNV calling precision/recall for a long-read variant caller such as Longshot. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-accuracy-evaluation-against-ground-truth))
- **snv-allele-observation-filtering**: Use when per-read allele calls at candidate biallelic SNV sites from long error-prone SMS reads (PacBio SMRT or ONT) must be filtered to phred-scaled allele quality &gt;= 7.0 before downstream phased genotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-allele-observation-filtering))
- **snv-annotated-reference-construction**: Use when preparing a truth-set diploid reference (two haploid FASTAs) from a reference genome plus a VCF, as the required input for diploid Linked-Read simulation with LRTK-SIM. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/snv-annotated-reference-construction))
- **snv-based-chimerism-genotype-assignment**: Use when post-transplant bone marrow (BMMC) or donor-mix scRNA-seq data are available as a Cell Ranger genome BAM, and you need per-cell host/donor genotype calls and mixture fractions from de novo SNVs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-based-chimerism-genotype-assignment))
- **snv-based-clonal-structure-validation**: Use when single-cell CNA clone assignments need orthogonal validation: paired single-cell + bulk WGS exists, somatic SNVs have been called (e.g. SCAN2/Mutect2), and an ARI between CNA- and SNV-based clusterings is the acceptance metric. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-based-clonal-structure-validation))
- **snv-caller-performance-tabulation**: Use when comparing the computational cost (wall-clock time, peak memory footprint) of SNV callers on ONT long-read BAMs, e.g. benchmarking miniSNV against Clair3 and Longshot on a Dorado sup-basecalled GIAB HG002 dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-caller-performance-tabulation))
- **snv-calling-and-haplotype-phasing**: Use when haplotype-resolved methylation or allele-specific methylation must be derived from PacBio HiFi/CCS modBAMs: per-read 5mCpG calls exist but reads are not yet haplotagged and no phased SNV VCF is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-calling-and-haplotype-phasing))
- **snv-calling-from-bulk-wgs**: Use when a cell-mixing single-cell experiment needs a ground-truth cell identity ground truth derived from cell line-specific SNVs, and a bulk WGS sample (FASTQ/BAM) is available for one of the mixed lines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/snv-calling-from-bulk-wgs))
- **snv-calling-from-metagenomic-read-mappings**: Use when sorted .bam read mappings to a reference .fasta are available and the goal is sensitive SNP detection with per-gene localization and synonymous/nonsynonymous classification for microdiversity or strain-sharing analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/snv-calling-from-metagenomic-read-mappings))
- **snv-calling-from-ont-long-reads**: Use when you have minimap2-aligned ONT (or PacBio SMRT) whole-genome BAM/CRAM reads plus an indexed GRCh38/hg38 FASTA and need accurate, phased diploid SNV calls benchmarked against GIAB high-confidence sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-calling-from-ont-long-reads))
- **snv-calling-from-read-mappings**: Use when per-sample BAMs of shotgun reads mapped to de novo signature genes are available and you need SNV-resolution phylogenies or mixed-allele statistics to stratify MAG clusters below species resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/snv-calling-from-read-mappings))
- **snv-calling-from-scrnaseq-reads**: Use when raw scRNA-seq reads (e.g. SRA files from a GEO study like the Kim dataset) need SNV genotyping per single cell, and no matched DNA-seq is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-calling-from-scrnaseq-reads))
- **snv-calling-from-single-cell-rnaseq**: Use when scRNA-seq data from a mixed-genotype or mixed-species sample (e.g. Jurkat/293T mixtures or transplant host/donor chimerism) must be deconvolved at single-cell resolution using sequence variation rather than gene expression markers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-calling-from-single-cell-rnaseq))
- **snv-callset-qc-filtering**: Use when raw breast cancer (GSE75688, SRP067248) or glioblastoma (EGAS00001001880) bulk WES and single-cell/bulk RNA-seq FASTQ files must be converted into QC-filtered somatic SNV read count matrices for Canopy2 input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-callset-qc-filtering))
- **snv-common-cpp**: Consumers explicitly select this release and their exact semantic owner libraries. No network listener, hidden discovery, automatic version selection or domain interpretation. ([QuanuX/Symphony](https://github.com/QuanuX/Symphony/tree/HEAD/libraries/snv-common-cpp) · ★2)
- **snv-engine**: Use `--descriptor` or receipt-bound `qxctl snv schema|template --owner OWNER --operation OP --prefix PREFIX --version 0.1.0-dev --json`. - **snv-expression-signature-cell-calling**: Use when single-cell RNA-seq data from a tumor sample needs per-cell tumor/normal labels and copy-number-based calling is unavailable or unreliable (e.g., normal-karyotype AML lacking CNAs), or when validating CNA-based calls against SNV/expression evidence. - **snv-false-positive-context-annotation**: Use when benchmarking long-read SNV calls against GIAB truth sets and you need to characterize error sequence contexts (indel-proximate FPs, homopolymer FNs) or evaluate an indel-proximity filter for FP reduction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-false-positive-context-annotation))
- **snv-fraction-cell-genotype-classification**: Use when scRNA-seq data from a mixed-cell or chimerism sample requires per-cell genotype assignment from transcriptome SNVs, especially when no distinguishing marker genes exist or marker-based calls need orthogonal validation. - **snv-gene-expression-linkage**: Use when you have per-cell SNV calls (e.g. .vcf-derived binary matrix) coupled with single-cell gene expression counts and need SNV-derived features that link genotype to expression phenotypes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-gene-expression-linkage))
- **snv-gene-linkage-modeling-with-lasso**: Use when you have a binary SNV matrix (n_cells x n_SNVs) and a gene expression matrix (n_cells x n_genes) from the same single cells and need to infer which eeSNVs are associated with expression of particular genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-gene-linkage-modeling-with-lasso))
- **snv-indel-precision-recall-evaluation**: Use when you need to quantify accuracy of germline SNV/indel calls (VCF) against a GIAB truth set, e.g. comparing a variant caller's performance on HG001/NA12878 using genotype-aware TP/FP/FN classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-indel-precision-recall-evaluation))
- **snv-judge**: Predict the pathogenicity of a human missense SNV (single nucleotide variant) using SNV-judge v5.2. - **snv-judge**: Predict the pathogenicity of a human missense single nucleotide variant (SNV) using SNV-judge v5 — a stacking ensemble (XGBoost + LightGBM + Isotonic calibration) trained on 2,000 ClinVar variants. Returns a calibrated probability (0–1), ACMG 5-tier classification (P / LP / VUS / LB / B), per-feature SHAP contributions, and optionally a full LLM-generated ACMG-style clinical interpretation report (Chinese / English / summary). Use this skill whenever a user asks to classify, score, interpret, or generate a clinical report for a genetic variant. - **snv-local-observer**: 1. Inspect the installed exact descriptor/schema/template before invoking. 2. - **snv-phase-block-chaining**: Use when ONT reads with MM/ML methylation tags have already been SNV-phased (HapCUT2/WhatsHap) into fragmented phase blocks and you need to bridge adjacent blocks using allele-specific methylation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-phase-block-chaining))
- **snv-phase-block-connection-verification**: Reference for benchmarking haplotype phasing accuracy when a method (e.g., MethPhaser) joins SNV phase blocks and you must validate each connection against a trio-phased truth VCF before reporting N50 or switch-error improvements. - **snv-short-indel-variant-calling**: Use when tumor WES/WGS and matched RNA-seq alignments must be converted into somatic SNV and short-indel VCFs as candidate neoantigen sources, with per-read-group parallel calling and Mutect2-learned filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/snv-short-indel-variant-calling))
- **snv-variant-calling-benchmarking**: Use when comparing single nucleotide variant caller performance on ONT long-read data (e.g., Dorado hac/sup-basecalled GIAB trio BAMs or Pbsim3-simulated reads) and per-caller precision, recall, and F1-score metrics are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-variant-calling-benchmarking))
- **snv-variant-calling-ont-reads**: Use when raw ONT R9/R10 long reads (e.g., HG002) have been basecalled with methylation tags and aligned, and phased SNV calls in VCF form are needed before haplotype phasing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/snv-variant-calling-ont-reads))

- **snx-code-quality**: The C rules of RFC #142: strict ISO C99, no redundant state and indices in place of pointer chains. - **snx-code-quality**: RFC #142's C rules: strict ANSI C, no redundant state, indices not pointer chains, one allocation per owner, atomics apart, no stored pointers. - **snx-code-quality**: RFC #142's C rules: strict ISO C99, no redundant state, indices not pointer chains, one allocation per owner, atomics apart, no stored pointers, one global struct per file. - **so-vs-vanilla**: Use when you need this Cognitive OS skill: A/B benchmark harness that measures Cognitive OS governance value by running the same task under full governance AND with all governance disabled (COS_DISABLE_ALL_GOVERNANCE=1).
